# Performance

Generated by `python3 scripts/bench.py report` from the current canonical measurement store in this repository. Do not edit numbers by hand.

Times are median process wall/CPU seconds; RSS is MiB. Ratios use the same model, instance, architecture, worker count, verdict (also distinct count outside default mode). Historical matches without a content digest are provisional; they cannot satisfy the current baseline cache. Unknown metadata stays unknown. Diagnostic samples, loaded or experimental A/B variants are excluded from headlines. Each number is named by the record it came from: the Evidence column carries that record's revision, instance, date and store file. The current measurement store ships with the sources ([benchmarking.md](benchmarking.md) section 6); the record links can be inspected in a fresh clone.
A tlx time is the median of the timed samples, and each timed sample is a separate process run that follows a discarded warm-up; the warm-up builds the native library once for the configuration, so the timed run does not pay for that build. The discarded run is not an isolated cold-cache measurement - the native cache is only rebuilt when the build is missing, and it is shared by the configurations of one batch ([benchmarking.md](benchmarking.md) section 1 owns the protocol); a cold-cache number needs the fresh-cache conditions the `ttfr` lane states. A TLC time is its one process, including its own start-up.
A row whose record names optimizations (`tlx optimizations` below: the run's own `Optimizations:` line, [usage.md](usage.md) section 6.3) explored a reduced search, so its ratio compares that reduced search with TLC's unreduced one and includes the reduction. The label is the record's own statement; a count difference is not used to infer it, and `-compile` rows carry it too. `none` means the row's samples name no optimization, `unknown` a row whose record carries no per-sample detail.
See [benchmark inputs and evidence](../bench/README.md) for verification and reproduction. Superseded measurements and backup archives are kept outside the published tree.

**Initial baseline, frozen 2026-10-11 at 08:51:23 +08:00.** This snapshot has 279 complete three-sample configurations out of 299. Later retries do not update it automatically. See [initial benchmark issues](issues.md) for correctness differences, errors, incomplete retries and the provenance of in-progress checkpoints.
